539. **Soil pollution and its control** (`biology/soil-pollution-and-its-control`) — The soil can be poisoned as surely as air and water: Kenya's Form 3 book gives soil pollution its own treatment — acid rain, aerosols with heavy metals, petroleum spills, inorganic fertilisers and solid waste — and its control, from compost pits to legislation, with radioactive emissions handled beside it. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Three Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-11 (Form 3), Ch. 2 Ecology, printed p. 63; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
540. **Cholera and typhoid — waterborne bacterial diseases** (`biology/cholera-and-typhoid-waterborne-bacterial-diseases`) — Two killer diseases travel in water and food: Kenya's Form 3 book teaches cholera (Vibrio cholerae) and typhoid (Salmonella typhi) together as human bacterial diseases — symptoms, oral rehydration salts, deep pit latrines, vaccination and food-handler hygiene. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Three Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-11 (Form 3), Ch. 2 Ecology, printed p. 65; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
541. **Parasitic worms — Ascaris and bilharzia** (`biology/parasitic-worms-ascaris-and-bilharzia`) — Two worms, two roads into the body: Kenya's Form 3 book teaches Ascaris lumbricoides — swallowed, migrating through liver, heart and lungs — and schistosomiasis (bilharzia), whose cercariae leave Biomphalaria and Bulinus snails to pierce the skin — each with its effects on the host, its adaptive characteristics, and its prevention. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Three Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-11 (Form 3), Ch. 2 Ecology, printed p. 69, printed p. 70; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
542. **Fruits — types and placentation** (`biology/fruits-types-and-placentation`) — A fruit is classified by what it is built from and how its seeds were attached: Kenya's Form 3 book works seed and fruit development (gibberellin-driven growth, parthenocarpy in pineapple and banana), the fruit kinds — berry, drupe, legume, capsule, schizocarp, caryopsis, cypsela, nut — false fruits, and the five placentation types, with a fruit-classification practical. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Three Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-11 (Form 3), Ch. 3 Reproduction in Plants and Animals, printed p. 100; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
543. **Seed dormancy and how it breaks** (`biology/seed-dormancy-and-how-it-breaks`) — A living seed may refuse to grow — and the refusal is useful: Kenya's Form 3 book teaches dormancy's value, its causes (immature embryo, abscisic-acid inhibitors, hard seed coats in wattle), and how it breaks (maturation, gibberellins and cytokinins, light, scarification — in agriculture by boiling, roasting and cracking wattle seed). (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Three Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-11 (Form 3), Ch. 4 Growth and Development, printed p. 138; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
544. **Primary and secondary growth in plants** (`biology/primary-and-secondary-growth-in-plants`) — A plant grows longer at its tips and thicker at its sides, by two different growths: Kenya's Form 3 book teaches primary growth (root and shoot apical meristems; zones of division, elongation and differentiation; an Indian-ink root-marking practical) and secondary growth (vascular and cork cambium, secondary xylem and phloem, bark, lenticels, annual rings and ageing a tree). (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Three Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-11 (Form 3), Ch. 4 Growth and Development, printed p. 143; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
545. **Insect moulting hormones** (`biology/insect-moulting-hormones`) — An insect's metamorphosis is decided by two hormones pulling opposite ways: Kenya's Form 3 book closes its growth chapter with juvenile hormone from the corpora allata, which holds the insect young, and ecdysone (moulting hormone) from the prothoracic glands, which drives the moult — and their balance decides larva, pupa or adult. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Three Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-11 (Form 3), Ch. 4 Growth and Development, printed p. 152; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
546. **Cloning and the human genome** (`biology/cloning-and-the-human-genome`) — Genetics ends by copying life and reading it whole: Kenya's Form 4 book teaches cloning step by step through Dolly the sheep, plant tissue culture producing new pyrethrum and banana varieties — both Kenyan cash crops — and the human genome: gene mapping and sequencing, with eugenics and the ethical issues of genetic engineering. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Four Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-12 (Form 4), Ch. 1 Genetics, printed p. 48; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
547. **Resistance — evolution in action** (`biology/resistance-evolution-in-action`) — Evolution you can watch within a lifetime: Kenya's Form 4 book teaches resistance — penicillin-breaking enzymes in bacteria, DDT-resistant mosquitoes, Plasmodium changing its coat antigens and resisting chloroquine, drug-resistant TB — as natural selection observed in action, in Kenyan health and agriculture. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Four Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-12 (Form 4), Ch. 2 Evolution, printed p. 75; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
548. **Support in plants — tissues and turgor** (`biology/support-in-plants-tissues-and-turgor`) — A plant stands up with water pressure and built-in girders: Kenya's Form 4 book opens its support chapter with the arrangement of tissues in stems — parenchyma and turgor, collenchyma, sclerenchyma fibres, xylem vessels and tracheids — herbaceous against woody stems, twining stems and tendrils, and a wilting-observation practical. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Four Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-12 (Form 4), Ch. 4 Support and Movement in Plants and Animals, printed p. 120; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
549. **Skeletons — hydrostatic, exoskeleton and endoskeleton** (`biology/skeletons-hydrostatic-exoskeleton-endoskeleton`) — Animals hold themselves up three ways: Kenya's Form 4 book compares the skeleton types — the hydrostatic skeleton of fluid under pressure, the exoskeleton of chitin outside the body (jointed, and shed in moulting/ecdysis), and the endoskeleton of living bone and cartilage inside — with a grasshopper, crab and millipede examination practical. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Four Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-12 (Form 4), Ch. 4 Support and Movement in Plants and Animals, printed p. 126; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
550. **Locomotion in a finned fish** (`biology/locomotion-in-a-finned-fish`) — A fish swims with muscle blocks, a gas float and a kit of fins: Kenya's Form 4 book teaches locomotion in the tilapia — streamlining, overlapping scales and mucus, myotomes, the swim bladder and buoyancy, the lateral line, and paired against unpaired fins — with a fish-examination practical and a tail-power calculation. (Kenya — *Secondary Biology Form Four Students' Book (Fifth Edition)*, student textbook, grade-12 (Form 4), Ch. 4 Support and Movement in Plants and Animals, printed p. 127; built for the Kenya placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kenya-placements.md)
551. **Diurnal and nocturnal organisms** (`biology/diurnal-and-nocturnal-organisms`) — Living things keep biological rhythms — some are active by day and some by night, and each is fitted to its time: flowers open and close at set hours, and nocturnal animals see in the dark with large eyes and a reflective eye layer. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a 6-a*, student textbook, Grade 6, 2.2 Diurnal and nocturnal organisms, printed p. 16; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
552. **Structural adaptations of plants to their environment** (`biology/structural-adaptations-of-plants-to-their-environment`) — A plant's structure is fitted to the place it lives — support structures hold it up, and in arid conditions a cactus turns its leaves into spines so it loses less water to evaporation. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a 6-a*, student textbook, Grade 6, 5.1 Structural adaptations of plants, printed p. 68; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
553. **Structural adaptations of animals to their environment** (`biology/structural-adaptations-of-animals-to-their-environment`) — An animal's body structures are fitted to its habitat — the camel carries a water reserve for dry land, and the aquatic turtle's locomotory structures are built for swimming. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a 6-a*, student textbook, Grade 6, 5.3 Structural adaptations of animals, printed p. 76; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
554. **Arachnids — spiders, ticks and scorpions** (`biology/arachnids-spiders-ticks-and-scorpions`) — Arachnids — spiders, ticks and scorpions — are arthropods whose body is built in two parts, cephalothorax and abdomen, living on land, in soil and in burrows, some of them parasitising plants and animals. (Moldova — *Biologie: Clasa a X-a (Profil real/umanist)*; *Biologie: manual pentru clasa a 7-a*, student textbook, Grade 7, 2.9 Class Arachnids, printed p. 44; Grade 10, Tema 4.18 Class Arachnids (Arachnida), printed p. 138; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
555. **Hygiene of breathing and first aid for asphyxia** (`biology/hygiene-of-breathing-and-first-aid-for-asphyxia`) — Breathing is kept safe by hygiene of the airways, and when breathing stops in asphyxia — caused by dust, drowning or strangling — first aid and artificial respiration are the response. (Moldova — *Biologie: manual pentru clasa a 7-a*, student textbook, Grade 7, 4.5 Hygiene of breathing, printed p. 105; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
556. **Hibernation, dormancy and active life** (`biology/hibernation-dormancy-and-active-life`) — Animals meet the cold season two ways — active life, or hibernation and torpor, in which the animal burns the fat it laid down in the warm season until conditions return. (Moldova — *Biologie: manual pentru clasa a 7-a*, student textbook, Grade 7, 5.1 Active life and hibernation, printed p. 136; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
557. **Animal migration** (`biology/animal-migration`) — Animals migrate — they travel between places as the seasons change — and the journeys have causes and set routes, from the “explosive” migration of lemmings to the migration schemes of salmon and eels. (Moldova — *Biologie: manual pentru clasa a 7-a*, student textbook, Grade 7, 5.2 Migrations in animals, printed p. 139; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
558. **Seasonal development cycles of plants — annual, biennial, perennial** (`biology/seasonal-development-cycles-of-plants-annual-biennial-perennial`) — A plant's seasonal development cycle is fitted to its lifespan: annual plants complete their life in one year, biennials in two years, and perennials live and develop over many years — biorhythms timed to the seasons. (Moldova — *Biologie: manual pentru clasa a 7-a*, student textbook, Grade 7, 5.3 Seasonal aspects of plant development cycles, printed p. 142; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
559. **Fruit formation and diversity of fruits** (`biology/fruit-formation-and-diversity-of-fruits`) — After fertilisation the flower's ovary becomes the fruit — the ovary wall forms the pericarp around the seeds — and fruits are diverse: drupes such as apricot, peach and sour cherry, pomes such as apple and pear, and dry multi-seeded fruits. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a VIII-a*, student textbook, Grade 8, 3.4 From flower to fruits and seeds; the fruit and diversity of fruits, printed p. 30; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
560. **Hygiene of the endocrine system** (`biology/hygiene-of-the-endocrine-system`) — The endocrine glands are kept healthy by hygiene: the risk factors and gland disorders are set out as rules, including diet restrictions for endocrine health. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a VIII-a*, student textbook, Grade 8, 4.9 Hygiene of the endocrine system, printed p. 62; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
561. **Investigating a local ecosystem near the school** (`biology/investigating-a-local-ecosystem-near-the-school`) — An ecosystem is investigated where it actually is: go out into an ecosystem from the school's area, observe it on a field excursion, and complete a trophic chain for that local ecosystem. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a VIII-a*, student textbook, Grade 8, 5.3 An ecosystem from the school's area, printed p. 124; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
562. **Biological science and research institutes in Moldova** (`biology/biological-science-and-research-institutes-in-moldova`) — Biological science in the Republic of Moldova is carried on in Academy institutes and university centres — microbiology, the genetics and plant-protection institute, the zoology institute and the National Botanical Garden — each with named research projects. (Moldova — *Biologie: Clasa a X-a (Profil real/umanist)*, student textbook, Grade 10, Tema 1.3 Development of the biological sciences in the Republic of Moldova, printed p. 9; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
563. **The vestibular analyser and balance** (`biology/the-vestibular-analyser-and-balance`) — Balance is sensed by the vestibular analyser, which controls static and dynamic balance and carries its signals along the vestibular nerve — the system whose disturbance appears as motion sickness. (Moldova — *Biologie — Procese și sisteme vitale: Manual pentru clasa a XI-a (Profil real / Profil umanist)*, student textbook, Grade 11, §11 The vestibular analyser, printed p. 37; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
564. **Hygiene and dysfunctions of the sensory system** (`biology/hygiene-and-dysfunctions-of-the-sensory-system`) — Every sensory analyser has its own hygiene and its own dysfunctions: risk factors are named analyser by analyser, and failures of sensation — anaesthesia as absence of cutaneous sensation, myopia — are studied as dysfunctions. (Moldova — *Biologie — Procese și sisteme vitale: Manual pentru clasa a XI-a (Profil real / Profil umanist)*, student textbook, Grade 11, §15 Hygiene and dysfunctions of the human sensory system, printed p. 48; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
565. **Hygiene, dysfunctions and diseases of the respiratory system** (`biology/hygiene-dysfunctions-and-diseases-of-the-respiratory-system`) — The respiratory system is protected by hygiene against its risk factors and diseases, and infectious patients cover mouth and nose and use strictly individual utensils, linen and objects so the disease does not spread. (Moldova — *Biologie — Procese și sisteme vitale: Manual pentru clasa a XI-a (Profil real / Profil umanist)*, student textbook, Grade 11, §32 Hygiene, dysfunctions and diseases of the respiratory system, printed p. 105; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
566. **Hygiene, dysfunctions and diseases of the urinary system** (`biology/hygiene-dysfunctions-and-diseases-of-the-urinary-system`) — The urinary system is kept healthy by hygiene, and its dysfunctions and diseases are recognised early — kidney-stone (calculi) pain is often confused with radiculitis, which delays treatment. (Moldova — *Biologie — Procese și sisteme vitale: Manual pentru clasa a XI-a (Profil real / Profil umanist)*, student textbook, Grade 11, §40 Hygiene, dysfunctions and diseases of the urinary system, printed p. 131; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
567. **Population characteristics and structure** (`biology/population-characteristics-and-structure`) — A population — living matter organised at its own level — is described by its characteristics, including its structure, and its sex structure is the ratio of females to males in it. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a 12-a*, student textbook, Grade 12, 4.2 Organisation of living matter: populations, printed p. 130; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
568. **Agroecosystems and sustainable agriculture** (`biology/agroecosystems-and-sustainable-agriculture`) — Agroecosystems are artificial ecosystems that began when sedentary life domesticated plants and animals, and sustainable agriculture keeps them productive with ecological technologies and rational crop rotation. (Moldova — *Biologie: Manual pentru clasa a 12-a*, student textbook, Grade 12, 4.5 Artificial ecosystems: agroecosystems, printed p. 143; built for the Moldova placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/moldova-placements.md)
569. **Bilateral symmetry and the coelom** (`biology/bilateral-symmetry-and-the-coelom`) — Bilateral symmetry — a body with two matching sides and a front end — is an evolutionary advance tied to active movement, and bilateral animals are graded by their body cavity: acoelomate (no coelom), pseudocoelomate (a false cavity) or coelomate (a true, lined coelom). (Cuba — *Programa Biología 2*, Grade 8, teacher programme, Unit 4, printed p. 17; *Biología 2 — Los animales y su conservación*, Grade 8, student textbook, 2024, Ch. 4, printed p. 58; *Biología 2*, Grade 8, student textbook, 2013 edition, Ch. 3, printed p. 54; built for the Cuba placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/cuba-placements.md)
570. **Avoiding adolescent pregnancy** (`biology/avoiding-adolescent-pregnancy`) — Adolescent pregnancy must be avoided — pregnancy while a young person is still developing carries health risks for the young mother and the baby and social consequences that can interrupt schooling and life plans, and healthy adolescent couple relationships are built on respect, responsibility and waiting. (Cuba — *Biología 3 — El organismo humano y la salud*, Grade 9, student textbook, 2025, Ch. 7, printed p. 340; built for the Cuba placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/cuba-placements.md)
571. **Planned immunisation — China's schedule** (`biology/planned-immunisation-chinas-schedule`) — Planned immunisation gives each vaccine at its set age on a national schedule — in China's, BCG and hepatitis B at birth — and the child's vaccination certificate is the document that holds the record, reproduced in the book from Beijing's two-page schedule. (China — *义务教育教科书《生物学》八年级下册 (Biology, Grade 8, Volume 2, People's Education Press, 2013)*, student textbook, §2, planned immunisation, printed pp. 83–84; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
572. **Health self-assessment — and mood** (`biology/health-self-assessment-and-mood`) — Health can be assessed, not assumed: a self-test scores body, mind and social adaptation — the WHO health triangle — against standards such as the Chinese school-age BMI cut-offs, and mood is regulated by three named methods: shift attention, vent appropriately, console yourself. (China — *义务教育教科书《生物学》八年级下册 (Biology, Grade 8, Volume 2, People's Education Press, 2013)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 §1 Evaluating your own health, printed p. 97; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
573. **Choosing a healthy lifestyle** (`biology/choosing-a-healthy-lifestyle`) — A healthy lifestyle is a plan a person designs and lives by — balanced meals, exercise, regular hours, no smoking or drinking, sensible screen time and group activities — and keeps long-term, not just for one week. (China — *义务教育教科书《生物学》八年级下册 (Biology, Grade 8, Volume 2, People's Education Press, 2013)*, student textbook, §2, design task, printed p. 108; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
574. **IVF and assisted reproduction** (`biology/ivf-and-assisted-reproduction`) — Assisted reproduction helps where natural conception fails — artificial insemination, IVF and embryo transfer, sperm and egg banks — the technology that gave the world Louise Brown in 1978 and mainland China its first test-tube baby in 1988, held under China's management measures and technical norms so embryo gene testing cannot slide into customised babies. (China — *义务教育教科书《生物学》七年级下册 (Biology, Grade 7, Volume 2, People's Education Press, 2012)*; *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修2 遗传与进化 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 2: Genetics and Evolution, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, §2 scientist story "'Father of the test-tube baby' wins the Nobel Prize", printed p. 13; §1 STS feature "Human assisted reproduction technology", printed p. 28; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
575. **Epigenetics — marks on the DNA** (`biology/epigenetics-marks-on-the-dna`) — Epigenetics is inheritance by marks on the DNA — methylation that switches a gene off without changing its base sequence — passed on without any change in the letters, as the changed toadflax flower and the agouti mouse's yellow-or-mottled coat show. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修2 遗传与进化 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 2: Genetics and Evolution, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, §2, epigenetic inheritance, printed p. 73; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
576. **Precision medicine — treatment by genome** (`biology/precision-medicine-treatment-by-genome`) — Precision medicine builds diagnosis and treatment on the patient's own genome: the same drug cures one patient and harms another, so targeted cancer drugs are chosen by the mutation present — and are useless, even harmful, without it — against the obstacles of data privacy, ethics and the sheer volume of sequence data. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修2 遗传与进化 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 2: Genetics and Evolution, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, §1 STS feature "Precision medicine", printed p. 86; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
577. **Radiometric dating — a geological clock** (`biology/radiometric-dating-a-geological-clock`) — Radiometric dating reads a rock's age from its radioactive clocks: uranium-238 halves in 4.5 billion years and carbon-14 in 5,730 years — which is why carbon-14 fails beyond about 70,000 years — and the lead–lead ratio dates a rock's formation. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修2 遗传与进化 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 2: Genetics and Evolution, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, §1 STS feature "An ideal 'geological clock'" and career, printed p. 105; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
578. **The nucleus — and its classic experiments** (`biology/the-nucleus-and-its-classic-experiments`) — The nucleus controls the cell, and its control role is proved by four classic experiments set side by side — axolotl nuclear transplant, salamander egg constriction, amoeba cutting and Acetabularia grafting — opened by a cloned-cow case, with chromatin against chromosome closing the account. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修1 分子与细胞 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 1: Molecules and Cells, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 §3 The nucleus, printed p. 54; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
579. **Models in biology — physical, conceptual, mathematical** (`biology/models-in-biology-physical-conceptual-mathematical`) — Biology thinks with models, and the models come in three kinds — physical models you can hold, conceptual models that organise ideas, and mathematical models that calculate — each judged by what it can and cannot show. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修1 分子与细胞 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 1: Molecules and Cells, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 §3 practical "Trying to build a three-dimensional model of a eukaryotic cell", printed p. 57; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
580. **Stem cells and totipotency** (`biology/stem-cells-and-totipotency`) — A totipotent cell can make a whole organism, and stem cells carry that power at every scale: Steward's carrot grown from one cell (1958), Dolly and the cloned monkeys Zhongzhong and Huahua from a body cell's nucleus, haematopoietic stem cells rebuilding blood, and iPS cells — a patient's own cells reprogrammed in 2006 — promising the same without an embryo and without rejection, the tumour risk stated plainly. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修1 分子与细胞 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 1: Molecules and Cells, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*; *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 feature "The world's first somatic-cell cloned monkeys", printed p. 58; Ch. 6 §2 Totipotency and stem cells, printed pp. 120–122; Ch. 2 §2 Stem cells, printed p. 46; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
581. **Channel proteins — aquaporins and ion channels** (`biology/channel-proteins-aquaporins-and-ion-channels`) — Channel proteins are fitted pores through the membrane — aquaporins pass water and ion channels pass particular ions — a whole class of membrane proteins with its own discovery story, from Agre's water channel (1988) to MacKinnon's potassium-channel structure (1998). (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修1 分子与细胞 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 1: Molecules and Cells, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 history feature "Humanity's exploration of channel proteins", printed p. 68; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
582. **ATP — the energy currency** (`biology/atp-the-energy-currency`) — ATP is the cell's energy currency: its terminal phosphate bond stores energy that is spent by transferring the phosphate onto the working protein itself, changing its shape — and the cell turns over a huge amount of ATP every day, about half a kilogram a minute in hard exercise. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修1 分子与细胞 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 1: Molecules and Cells, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 5 §2 ATP, the cell's energy "currency", printed p. 86; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
583. **Autophagy — the cell eats itself** (`biology/autophagy-the-cell-eats-itself`) — In autophagy the cell digests its own damaged parts inside lysosomes — survival rations in starvation and a clearance service for invading microbes and worn organelles in damage and ageing. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》必修1 分子与细胞 (Senior High Biology, Compulsory Book 1: Molecules and Cells, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 6 §3 concept box "Autophagy", printed p. 126; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
584. **The internal environment — the fluid cells live in** (`biology/the-internal-environment-the-fluid-cells-live-in`) — Every cell lives in fluid: body fluid divides into the intracellular two-thirds and the extracellular third — plasma, tissue fluid and lymph — whose composition and osmotic pressure (about 770 kPa at 37 °C, over 90% of it from Na⁺ and Cl⁻) the body holds steady, a salt solution much like the seawater life came from. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 1 §1 The environment cells live in, printed p. 2; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
585. **Adult neurogenesis — new neurons in the grown brain** (`biology/adult-neurogenesis-new-neurons-in-the-grown-brain`) — New neurons are born in the adult brain — in the hippocampus and the olfactory bulb among other places — against the old dogma that brain cells only die, and running, experience and stress can raise or lower that birth rate. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §1 feature "Do brain cells really only die?", printed p. 21; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
586. **Brain science — mapping the brain** (`biology/brain-science-mapping-the-brain`) — Brain science maps the brain three ways — its cell types, its connections and its activity — as national research programmes like the China Brain Project (launched 2018) set out to know the brain, simulate the brain, protect the brain and develop with the brain. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §4 feature "The China Brain Project", printed p. 36; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
587. **The language areas of the cortex** (`biology/the-language-areas-of-the-cortex`) — Language lives in four areas of the left cerebral cortex — W, V, H and S — each found by the deficit its damage leaves: the patient who understands but cannot speak, and the patient who speaks but cannot understand, map them before the map is shown. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §5 Language, printed p. 37; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
588. **How memory works — four stages** (`biology/how-memory-works-four-stages`) — Memory works in four stages — sensory memory under a second, first-level memory of seconds to minutes (the verification code you forget at once), second-level memory of minutes to years, and third-level memory that may be permanent — and study works with the mechanism: repeat, and bind new information to old. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §5 Learning and memory, printed p. 38; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
589. **Brain–computer interfaces** (`biology/brain-computer-interfaces`) — A brain–computer interface reads the brain's signals and lets them control a machine directly — implanted arrays give stronger signals at the cost of surgery, while scalp EEG needs no surgery but reads weaker signals. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §5 feature "Brain–computer interfaces: tools that obey the mind", printed p. 40; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
590. **Hormone products — benefits and risks** (`biology/hormone-products-benefits-and-risks`) — Hormone products must be weighed case by case — the chemistry that treats dwarfism and diabetes also breeds fish, fattens livestock, masks fever and endangers athletes — so each of the book's ten real cases is judged on its own evidence. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 §2 evaluation feature "Weighing the use of hormone products", printed p. 56; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
591. **The brain's lymphatic vessels — a 2015 discovery** (`biology/the-brains-lymphatic-vessels-a-2015-discovery`) — The brain was the one place the immune system was thought absent — until 2015, when lymphatic vessels were found in mouse meninges, and imaged in humans in 2017, hidden by hugging a great blood vessel through a region MRI reads badly: a lesson in how much anatomy can still be missed. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 §1 feature "New discoveries about the immune system", printed p. 70; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
592. **Cancer immunotherapy — checkpoints and engineered T cells** (`biology/cancer-immunotherapy-checkpoints-and-engineered-t-cells`) — Cancer immunotherapy fights a tumour with the patient's own immune system: T cells taken out, re-engineered to recognise the cancer, multiplied and returned — or checkpoint blockade that stops the tumour hiding behind PD-L1's 'self' signal to the T cell's PD-1. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 §2 feature "Immunotherapy for cancer", printed p. 76; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
593. **Organ transplantation — and HLA matching** (`biology/organ-transplantation-and-hla-matching`) — A transplanted organ carries its donor's HLA identity labels, so matching donor to recipient — over half the main HLA shared can suffice — and suppressing rejection decide whether the graft lives; scarce organs are allocated by urgency and match, in China by the computerised allocation system, not by influence. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 §4 Organ transplantation, printed p. 83; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
594. **Plant growth regulators in farming** (`biology/plant-growth-regulators-in-farming`) — Plant growth regulators are artificial partners to the plant hormones — in China regulated as pesticides under rules from 1989 onward, sold only when registered — and each farming case, from gibberellin making barley produce amylase to fruit expanders and sprout suppressants, is judged on its benefit and its health questions. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 5 §3 Plant growth regulators, printed pp. 100–103; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
595. **Controlling flowering for people** (`biology/controlling-flowering-for-people`) — Flowering time can be shaped for people — by temperature, day length, growth regulators and cultivation — so flowers and fruit arrive on the festival day and their seasons spread across the market. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修1 稳态与调节 (Senior High Biology, Selective Compulsory Book 1: Homeostasis and Regulation, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 5 §4 feature "Shaping flowering time to create a beautiful life", printed p. 109; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
596. **Ecological engineering — designing with nature** (`biology/ecological-engineering-designing-with-nature`) — Ecological engineering designs or repairs ecosystems on ecological principles — self-generation, cycling, coordination and wholeness — for less consumption, more benefit and sustainability, as the classical mulberry–silkworm–fishpond and modern village biogas, lake-restoration and mining-land systems show. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修2 生物与环境 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 2: Biology and Environment, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 1 scientist story: Ma Shijun （马世骏）, printed p. 12; Ch. 4 §3 Ecological engineering and its four principles, printed p. 98; Ch. 4 §3 Ecological engineering in practice, printed p. 104; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
597. **Community structure — layers, seasons and richness** (`biology/community-structure-layers-seasons-and-richness`) — A community has a structure: its species are layered vertically, patterned horizontally and changed with the seasons, and its species richness — how many species it holds — is its measure. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修2 生物与环境 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 2: Biology and Environment, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §1 The structure of a community, printed p. 26; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
598. **Information transfer in ecosystems** (`biology/information-transfer-in-ecosystems`) — Ecosystems run on information as well as energy and matter: physical, chemical and behavioural signals pass from a source through a channel to a receptor — the bee's dances, the silkworm moth's bombykol, a plant's phytochrome reading light, and the volatiles an attacked tobacco plant releases to summon parasitoids. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修2 生物与环境 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 2: Biology and Environment, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 §4 Information transfer, printed p. 68; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
599. **Ecosystem stability — resistance and resilience** (`biology/ecosystem-stability-resistance-and-resilience`) — An ecosystem in balance holds three balances — structure, function and input–output — and its stability comes in two kinds: resistance stability, withstanding a disturbance, and resilience stability, recovering from one, rainforest and Arctic tundra standing at the two ends. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修2 生物与环境 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 2: Biology and Environment, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 §5 Ecological balance and stability, printed p. 73; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
600. **The ecological footprint** (`biology/the-ecological-footprint`) — The ecological footprint converts everything a person, city or country consumes and discards into the land and water area that must supply and absorb it, in six components — cropland, grazing land, forest, fishing grounds, built-up land and the carbon footprint. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修2 生物与环境 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 2: Biology and Environment, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 §1 The ecological footprint, printed p. 82; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
601. **The values of biodiversity — direct, indirect, potential** (`biology/the-values-of-biodiversity-direct-indirect-potential`) — Biodiversity — held at three levels, genetic, species and ecosystem — has value in three kinds: direct value in food, fibres and medicines such as artemisinin, indirect value in carbon fixed, soil and floodwater held and climate steadied, stated to far exceed the direct, and potential value in the uses nobody knows yet. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修2 生物与环境 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 2: Biology and Environment, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 §2 The value of biodiversity, printed p. 90; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
602. **Ecological ethics — who nature is for** (`biology/ecological-ethics-who-nature-is-for`) — Ecological ethics asks who nature is for — anthropocentrism, the view that nature exists for humans, argued against its rivals — and China's wildlife reserves, railway culverts for antelope and village eco-compensation are that argument made practical. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修2 生物与环境 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 2: Biology and Environment, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 §2 STS: ecological ethics, printed p. 97; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
603. **Growing microbes — media, pure culture and counting** (`biology/growing-microbes-media-pure-culture-and-counting`) — Microbes are grown on media built from a carbon source, a nitrogen source, salts and water, tuned for pH and oxygen, and a pure culture is won by streak plate or dilution spread plate — then counted by colonies on the 30–300 rule, knowing the count runs low because chained and clumped cells count once. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 1 §2 Culturing microbes, printed pp. 9–18; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
604. **Screening microbes at scale — high-throughput screening** (`biology/screening-microbes-at-scale-high-throughput`) — High-throughput screening searches the microbial kingdom at scale: thousands of mutagenised strains cultured in microplates and micro-bioreactors under automated handling and computer analysis — how industry searches a kingdom of which under 1% can yet be cultured. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 1 §2 feature "High-throughput screening of microbial strains", printed p. 15; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
605. **Fermentation engineering — from flask to factory** (`biology/fermentation-engineering-from-flask-to-factory`) — Fermentation engineering runs microbes at factory scale as one chain — strain selection and breeding, scale-up culture, medium design, sterilisation, inoculation, the computer-monitored fermentation itself, then separation and purification — and the conditions steer what the microbes make, as glutamate fermentation's pH switch proves. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 1 §3 Fermentation engineering, printed p. 22; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
606. **Plant somatic hybridisation — fusing cells across species** (`biology/plant-somatic-hybridisation-fusing-cells-across-species`) — Plant somatic hybridisation fuses whole cells across species: walls removed by cellulase and pectinase, protoplasts fused by PEG, high-Ca²⁺/high-pH or an electric pulse, and the hybrid cell grown through callus into a plant — the tomato–potato that did neither job well told honestly among China's citrus successes. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §1 Plant somatic hybridisation, printed p. 36; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
607. **Plant cell engineering at work** (`biology/plant-cell-engineering-at-work`) — Plant cell engineering at work: micropropagation multiplying orchids and medicinal plants in tubes, virus-free stock from shoot-tip culture yielding over 50% more potatoes, haploid breeding through anther culture, useful mutants selected from cultured cells, and cell-product factories growing shikonin, taxol and ginseng saponins. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §1 Applications of plant cell engineering, printed p. 39; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
608. **Animal cell culture — conditions and passage** (`biology/animal-cell-culture-conditions-and-passage`) — Animal cells can be grown outside the body in a dish, if the culture gives them a suitable medium with serum, sterility, body temperature, the right pH and the right gases — and a grown culture is passaged by trypsinising it into single cells that attach and divide again. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §2 Animal cell culture, printed p. 43; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
609. **Monoclonal antibodies — the hybridoma trick** (`biology/monoclonal-antibodies-the-hybridoma-trick`) — A monoclonal antibody is one antibody made in bulk by one clone: fuse the single B cell that makes it to an endlessly dividing myeloma cell, select and clone the hybridoma, and it makes that one antibody for as long as it lives — the trick behind pregnancy test kits and antibody medicines. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §2 Cell fusion and monoclonal antibodies, printed p. 48; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
610. **Embryo engineering — fertilisation to transfer** (`biology/embryo-engineering-fertilisation-to-transfer`) — Embryo engineering works with the early embryo: sperm capacitation and fertilisation with its two blocks to polyspermy, cleavage to the morula and the blastocyst with its inner cell mass, then IVF, embryo transfer and embryo splitting to multiply valuable animals — and to save species down to their last females. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 2 §3 Embryo engineering, printed p. 56; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
611. **Protein engineering — designing proteins backwards** (`biology/protein-engineering-designing-proteins-backwards`) — Protein engineering runs the central dogma in reverse: from the function wanted, design the protein's structure, read off the amino-acid sequence, then build the gene — the second-generation genetic engineering that made fast-acting insulin analogues and heat-proofed T4 lysozyme. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 3 §4 Protein engineering, printed p. 93; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
612. **Biological weapons — and the ban** (`biology/biological-weapons-and-the-ban`) — Biological weapons are pathogenic bacteria, viruses and toxins used to harm people, and the world has banned them — the 1972 Convention, which China joined in 1984, forbids developing, producing or stockpiling them, a position China reaffirms: never develop, produce or stockpile, and oppose proliferation. (China — *普通高中教科书《生物学》选择性必修3 生物技术与工程 (Senior High Biology, Elective Compulsory Book 3: Biotechnology and Engineering, People's Education Press, 2019-standard edition)*, student textbook, Ch. 4 §3 Banning biological weapons, printed p. 111; built for the China placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/china-placements.md)
613. **Adaptations — how organisms fit their environment** (`biology/adaptations-how-organisms-fit-their-environment`) — Organisms carry adaptations — features that fit them to their environment — as Greece's Mediterranean plants show: pine and olive hold small, hard, needle-like leaves against dry summers while the plane tree by the water spreads broad leaves, so the same life function is fitted differently in dry and wet habitats. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ & Β΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia A' & B' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grade 7*; *Βιολογία Α΄ Γυμνασίου — Τετράδιο Εργασιών (Workbook), Grade 7*, student textbook, §1.4, printed p. 30; Activity 1.6, printed p. 17; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
614. **Open and closed circulatory systems** (`biology/open-and-closed-circulatory-systems`) — Animals move substances two ways: in an open circulatory system the blood leaves the vessels and bathes the tissues directly, while in a closed system it never leaves the vessels and capillaries do the exchanging — and some simple animals, like jellyfish, manage with no system at all, exchanging by diffusion. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ & Β΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia A' & B' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grade 7*, student textbook, §3.3, printed p. 61; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
615. **Gas-exchange organs across animals** (`biology/gas-exchange-organs-across-animals`) — Animals exchange gases through organs fitted to where they live — skin in worms and frogs, feathery gills in water animals, branching tracheae opening at spiracles in insects, and lungs in land vertebrates — all of them thin, moist, wide-surfaced and well supplied. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ & Β΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia A' & B' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grade 7*; *Βιολογία Α΄ Γυμνασίου — Τετράδιο Εργασιών (Workbook), Grade 7*, student textbook, §4.3, printed p. 81; Activities 4.4–4.7, printed p. 55; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
616. **Movement in unicellular organisms** (`biology/movement-in-unicellular-organisms`) — Single-celled organisms move with what they have: an amoeba flows by pseudopodia — temporary feet of streaming cytoplasm — while others row with many short cilia or lash one or two long flagella, each method fitted to life at microscopic size. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ & Β΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia A' & B' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grade 7*, student textbook, §5.1, printed p. 98; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
617. **Food additives and preservatives** (`biology/food-additives-and-preservatives`) — Foods carry additives — preservatives that stop microbes and spoilage, colours, flavours and others — each with its job and its code on the label, and reading the label is how a buyer knows what is in their plate, Greece's workbook's own cry: 'Help! A preservative in my plate'. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ Γυμνασίου — Τετράδιο Εργασιών (Workbook), Grade 7*, workbook, Activity 2.7, printed p. 33; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
618. **Artificial respiration — first aid for breathing** (`biology/artificial-respiration-and-first-aid-for-breathing`) — Artificial respiration is rescue breathing: when a person's own breathing has stopped, another person breathes air into their lungs in a steady rhythm to keep oxygen reaching the blood until help comes or their own breathing returns. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ Γυμνασίου — Τετράδιο Εργασιών (Workbook), Grade 7*, workbook, Activity 4.12, printed p. 67; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
619. **Animal tissues — epithelial, connective, muscle, nervous** (`biology/animal-tissues-epithelial-connective-muscle-nervous`) — An animal's cells are organised into four tissue kinds — epithelial tissue covering and lining, connective tissue joining and supporting, muscle tissue moving, and nervous tissue carrying messages — each kind built of cells fitted to its one job. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ Λυκείου (Biologia A' Lykeiou), Grade 10*; *Βιολογία Β΄ & Γ΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia B' & C' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grades 8–9*; *Βιολογία Β΄ & Γ΄ Γυμνασίου — Εργαστηριακός Οδηγός (Laboratory Guide), Grades 8–9*, student textbook, §1.3, printed p. 29; Exercise 4, printed p. 25; Ch. 1, printed p. 9; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
620. **Alleles — dominant and recessive forms of a gene** (`biology/alleles-dominant-and-recessive-forms-of-a-gene`) — A gene comes in alternative forms called alleles, and when two different alleles meet, the dominant one shows in the organism while the recessive one is carried hidden — the distinction Greece's Grades 8–9 book teaches as its own section before Mendel's laws. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ & Γ΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia B' & C' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grades 8–9*, student textbook, §5.3, printed p. 103; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
621. **Genetically modified organisms** (`biology/genetically-modified-organisms`) — A genetically modified organism carries a gene deliberately moved into it — transgenic plants and animals made for production, which Greece's books teach as a named topic — with the new trait inherited by its offspring like any of its own genes. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ & Γ΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia B' & C' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grades 8–9*; *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, §6.2, printed p. 124; Ch. 9, printed p. 137; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
622. **The European Code Against Cancer** (`biology/the-european-code-against-cancer`) — The European Code Against Cancer is a short public list of everyday rules — do not smoke, keep a healthy weight, move daily, eat well, limit alcohol, respect the sun, and take the offered screenings and vaccinations — reproduced in Greece's Grades 8–9 appendix as the Greek and European way of teaching cancer prevention. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ & Γ΄ Γυμνασίου (Biologia B' & C' Gymnasiou) — Vivlio Mathiti, Grades 8–9*, student textbook, Appendix, printed p. 142; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
623. **Cancer — what it is and how it starts** (`biology/cancer-what-it-is-and-how-it-starts`) — Cancer begins when mutations strike the genes that control cell multiplication, so a cell divides without control and its descendants form a tumour — and Greece teaches it with its causes, its prevention and the European Code Against Cancer alongside. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ & Γ΄ Γυμνασίου — Τετράδιο Εργασιών (Workbook), Grades 8–9*; *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*; *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, workbook, Unit 4, Act. 3, printed p. 47; §1.4, printed p. 53; Ch. 6, printed p. 104; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
624. **The genetic code** (`biology/the-genetic-code`) — The genetic code is the dictionary of life: bases are read in threes — codons — each codon naming one amino acid, with AUG as the start signal and three codons as stops; the code is nearly universal, the standing proof that all life shares one ancestry and one language. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ & Γ΄ Γυμνασίου — Τετράδιο Εργασιών (Workbook), Grades 8–9*; *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*; *Βιολογία Γ΄ Λυκείου — Εργαστηριακός Οδηγός, Τεύχος Β΄ (Laboratory Guide B), Grade 12*, workbook, Unit 5, Act. 4 + Appendix, printed p. 71; Ch. 2, printed p. 38; Exercise 2, printed p. 23; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
625. **Blood vessels — arteries, veins and capillaries** (`biology/blood-vessels-arteries-veins-and-capillaries`) — The blood travels in three kinds of vessel — arteries carrying it away from the heart in thick elastic walls, capillaries with walls one cell thick where exchange happens, and veins carrying it back with valves against the low pressure — each kind built for its part of the journey. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ Λυκείου (Biologia A' Lykeiou), Grade 10*, student textbook, Ch. 3, printed p. 47; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
626. **Bone formation and growth** (`biology/bone-formation-and-growth`) — Bones form by ossification — cartilage or membrane is gradually replaced by hard bone — and a long bone keeps growing in length at its growth plates until adulthood, while thickening and repair continue through life. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ Λυκείου (Biologia A' Lykeiou), Grade 10*, student textbook, Ch. 7, printed p. 115; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
627. **Disorders of the muscular system** (`biology/disorders-of-the-muscular-system`) — The muscular system has its own named disorders — strains and cramps from overuse and diseases that waste or weaken the muscle fibres themselves — taught in Greece's Grade 10 book as their own section, with care and prevention. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ Λυκείου (Biologia A' Lykeiou), Grade 10*, student textbook, Ch. 8, printed p. 132; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
628. **The autonomic nervous system** (`biology/the-autonomic-nervous-system`) — The autonomic nervous system runs the body's automatic life — heartbeat, digestion, pupil size, sweating — through two opposing branches, sympathetic speeding and preparing for action, parasympathetic slowing and restoring, without any conscious command. (Greece — *Βιολογία Α΄ Λυκείου (Biologia A' Lykeiou), Grade 10*, student textbook, Ch. 9, printed p. 165; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
629. **Factors that affect human health** (`biology/factors-that-affect-human-health`) — Human health is shaped by three families of factors together — the environment a person lives in, their lifestyle and habits, and the heredity they are born with — the frame Greece's Grade 11 book opens its health unit with. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*, student textbook, §1.1, printed p. 9; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
630. **Non-specific defence — barriers and inflammation** (`biology/non-specific-defence-barriers-and-inflammation`) — The body's first defence is non-specific — it answers any invader the same way: skin and membranes as barriers, inflammation flooding an injured place with blood, fever raising the temperature, and phagocyte cells engulfing microbes whole. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*, student textbook, §1.3.1, printed p. 31; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
631. **Specific defence and immunity** (`biology/specific-defence-and-immunity`) — Specific defence is immunity proper: the body recognises an antigen as foreign and answers with antibodies and dedicated cells — active immunity made by the body itself, lasting with memory, and passive immunity borrowed ready-made, as from mother to baby. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*, student textbook, §1.3.2, printed p. 34; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
632. **When the immune system goes wrong** (`biology/when-the-immune-system-goes-wrong`) — The immune system itself can be the problem: allergies are its over-reaction to harmless things, autoimmune diseases its attack on the body's own tissues, and immunodeficiency its failure to defend at all — Greece's Grade 11 book gives the failures their own named section. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*, student textbook, §1.3.3, printed p. 40; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
633. **Productivity of ecosystems** (`biology/productivity-of-ecosystems`) — An ecosystem's productivity is the rate at which it builds living matter: primary production by its plants — gross, before their own respiration is subtracted, and net, after — and secondary production by the animals that eat them, the measure that decides how much life a place can support. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*, student textbook, §2.2.3, printed p. 78; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
634. **Desertification** (`biology/desertification`) — Desertification is fertile land turning to desert under overgrazing, over-cultivation, tree loss and a drying climate — a named Mediterranean problem in Greece's Grade 11 book, fought by careful grazing, tree planting and care of water. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*, student textbook, §2.4.3, printed p. 101; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
635. **Human population diversity** (`biology/human-population-diversity`) — Human populations vary — in skin colour, blood groups, body build and a thousand inherited traits — but the variation runs within one single species and mostly inside every population, so biology finds no separate human races, only one variable kind. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Γενικού Λυκείου (Biologia B' Lykeiou), Grade 11*, student textbook, §3.4.7, printed p. 151; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
636. **Pollutants and cell life** (`biology/pollutants-and-cell-life`) — Pollutants act at the level of the cell: Greece's Grade 11 laboratory guide tests their effects on living cells directly — growth, division and survival change with what the cells are exposed to — which is how the damage pollution does to whole organisms begins. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Λυκείου Γενικής Παιδείας — Εργαστηριακός Οδηγός (Laboratory Guide), Grade 11*, student laboratory guide, Exercise 6, printed p. 41; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
637. **The greenhouse effect** (`biology/the-greenhouse-effect`) — The greenhouse effect is the atmosphere's blanket: sunlight warms the Earth, the Earth radiates heat back, and greenhouse gases absorb part of that heat instead of letting it escape — Greece's Grade 11 laboratory guide detects it qualitatively and quantitatively in a model experiment with warmed, measured air. (Greece — *Βιολογία Β΄ Λυκείου Γενικής Παιδείας — Εργαστηριακός Οδηγός (Laboratory Guide), Grade 11*, student laboratory guide, Exercise 8, printed p. 49; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
638. **Cell signalling — the membrane as receiver** (`biology/cell-signalling-the-membrane-as-receiver`) — The plasma membrane is also a receiver: it carries receptor proteins that catch message molecules from outside the cell and pass the signal inward, so the cell answers hormones and other signals without the messenger entering. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §2.2, printed p. 55; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
639. **The endomembrane system** (`biology/the-endomembrane-system`) — The endomembrane system is the cell's inner production line: the endoplasmic reticulum makes proteins and lipids, vesicles carry them to the Golgi apparatus for finishing and addressing, and lysosomes digest — membranes flowing into one another as one working system. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §2.3, printed p. 61; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
640. **The cytoskeleton** (`biology/the-cytoskeleton`) — The cytoskeleton is the cell's inner scaffolding of protein fibres — microtubules, microfilaments and intermediate filaments — holding its shape, moving its organelles and pulling its chromosomes apart in division. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §2.3, printed p. 66; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
641. **Enzyme cofactors and coenzymes** (`biology/enzyme-cofactors-and-coenzymes`) — Many enzymes work only with a helper: a cofactor — often a metal ion — or an organic coenzyme, many of them made from vitamins, sits with the enzyme and takes part in the reaction it speeds. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §3.2, printed p. 87; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
642. **Factors that affect the rate of photosynthesis** (`biology/factors-that-affect-the-rate-of-photosynthesis`) — The rate of photosynthesis is set by limiting factors — light intensity, carbon dioxide concentration and temperature — and whichever is scarcest caps the rate, so raising the others alone does nothing until the scarcest is raised. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §3.3, printed p. 101; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
643. **Photosynthesis in prokaryotes** (`biology/photosynthesis-in-prokaryotes`) — Photosynthesis began in prokaryotes: cyanobacteria run it much as plants do and filled the air with oxygen, while other bacteria photosynthesise without releasing oxygen, using sulphur compounds instead of water — the process is older than the chloroplast, which is itself a captured prokaryote. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §3.3, printed p. 102; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
644. **Energy from the breakdown of lipids and proteins** (`biology/energy-from-the-breakdown-of-lipids-and-proteins`) — Cells also burn lipids and proteins for energy: fats are split into fatty acids and glycerol that enter respiration's pathways — gram for gram fat yields more than carbohydrate — and amino acids enter after their nitrogen is removed, tying energy release back to photosynthesis as the source of it all. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §3.4, printed p. 113; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
645. **The central dogma of molecular biology** (`biology/the-central-dogma-of-molecular-biology`) — The central dogma states the flow of genetic information: DNA is transcribed into RNA and RNA is translated into protein, while DNA copies itself — information flows from the archive to the working copy to the machine — and Greece's Grade 12 volume opens its molecular genetics with it. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Α΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume A)*, student textbook, §4.2, printed p. 123; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
646. **Organisation of the genetic material** (`biology/organisation-of-the-genetic-material`) — Genetic material is organised very differently across life: a prokaryote keeps one circular DNA molecule loose in the cytoplasm, while a eukaryote winds its DNA with proteins into chromosomes inside a nucleus — and viruses carry theirs as DNA or RNA in a protein coat. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 1, printed p. 22; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
647. **DNA in mitochondria and chloroplasts** (`biology/dna-in-mitochondria-and-chloroplasts`) — Mitochondria and chloroplasts carry their own small circular DNA — a leftover of their origin as free bacteria — so they make some of their own proteins and, in animals, are inherited through the egg alone. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 1, printed p. 25; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
648. **Gene regulation — switching genes on and off** (`biology/gene-regulation-switching-genes-on-and-off`) — Cells switch genes on and off: prokaryotes group genes in operons a repressor can shut, and eukaryotes control expression at many levels, so the same genome makes a nerve cell and a liver cell — regulation is how one instruction book runs every cell kind. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 2, printed p. 44; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
649. **Genomic and cDNA libraries** (`biology/genomic-and-cdna-libraries`) — A genomic library stores a whole genome as cloned DNA fragments in bacteria — every gene shelved — while a cDNA library is built from a cell's messenger RNA copied back into DNA, so it shelves only the genes that cell was using; a labelled probe finds the wanted clone by hybridisation. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 4, printed p. 61; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
650. **Lethal alleles** (`biology/lethal-alleles`) — Some alleles are lethal: in two copies — or one, for a dominant lethal — they kill the organism, often before birth, which is why certain crosses give surviving offspring in a 2:1 ratio instead of Mendel's 3:1. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 5, printed p. 80; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
651. **Microbial cultures and industrial fermentation** (`biology/microbial-cultures-and-industrial-fermentation`) — Biotechnology's working tool is the microbial culture: microbes grown pure in the lab, then scaled up in great fermenters with food, air and stirring controlled, their products separated and purified — penicillin production stands as the milestone that proved the scale. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 7, printed p. 112; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
652. **Vaccines made by genetic engineering** (`biology/vaccines-made-by-genetic-engineering`) — Genetic engineering makes vaccines by moving a pathogen's antigen gene into a safe microbe, which then manufactures the antigen in bulk — the body learns the invader from that one part, without ever meeting the disease itself. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 8, printed p. 125; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
653. **Agrobacterium — plant gene transfer** (`biology/agrobacterium-plant-gene-transfer`) — The soil bacterium Agrobacterium naturally carries genes into plant cells on its plasmid, and genetic engineers use that same delivery system to give crop plants new genes. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 9, printed p. 137; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
654. **Pharming — proteins from transgenic animals** (`biology/pharming-proteins-from-transgenic-animals`) — Pharming makes transgenic animals secrete human pharmaceutical proteins in their milk — a medicine factory on four legs — so the protein is harvested by milking, purified, and used to treat people. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 9, printed p. 141; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
655. **Gene patents — and owning a discovery** (`biology/gene-patents-and-owning-a-discovery`) — A gene patent claims a discovered gene sequence as intellectual property, and Greece's Grade 12 bioethics chapter asks the hard question outright: can discovering a gene — finding what nature already made — fairly be owned, and what does owning it do to medicine and research. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Ch. 12, printed p. 173; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
656. **Techniques of molecular biology** (`biology/techniques-of-molecular-biology`) — Molecular biology works through a bench of named techniques — extracting and cutting DNA, separating fragments by gel electrophoresis, multiplying them by PCR, reading their sequence and joining them anew — and Greece's Grade 12 appendix gathers twenty pages of them as the toolkit behind every genetics chapter. (Greece — *Βιολογία Γ΄ Γενικού Λυκείου — Τεύχος Β΄ (Grade 12 orientation volume B)*, student textbook, Appendix, printed p. 179; built for the Greece placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/greece-placements.md)
657. **Protected areas and the Red Book of Kazakhstan** (`biology/protected-areas-and-the-red-book-of-kazakhstan`) — Kazakhstan guards its nature through a network of specially protected areas — state reserves, national parks and sanctuaries, each with its own rules — and lists its rarest species, the snow leopard, the Caspian seal and Selevinia among them, in the Red Book of Kazakhstan, protected by law, by science and by practical measures. (Kazakhstan — *BIOLOGY 7 (Sagintayev et al., Astana-kitap), English-language edition (record 1582)*; *Biology Grade 9 Student's Book (O'Callaghan et al., Express Publishing) (record 475)*; *Biology — Grade 8 Student's Book (O'Callaghan et al., Express Publishing) (record 368)*; *БИОЛОГИЯ 7 (Solovyova et al., Atamura, first edition 2017), Kazakh-language edition (record 245)*; *Биология 7 (Ochkur & Kurmanalieva, Mektep, first edition 2017), Kazakh-language edition (record 246)*; *Биология 7 (Ochkur et al., Mektep 2025), Kazakh-language edition (record 1476)*; *Биология 7 (Solovyova, Atamura 2025), Russian-language edition (record 1519)*; *Биология — 7-сынып (NIS Biology, Grade 7), Kazakh-language edition (record 1039)*, student textbook, §1.6 Specially protected natural areas of Kazakhstan, printed p. 16; §6 Specially protected natural territories of Kazakhstan, printed p. 26; §7 The Red Book of Kazakhstan, printed p. 30; §6 Specially protected areas in Kazakhstan. Protected areas of the local region, printed p. 28; §7 The Red Book of the Republic of Kazakhstan, printed p. 33; 1.5 Protection of animals and plants, printed p. 18; §6 Specially protected natural territories in Kazakhstan, printed p. 23; §7 The Red Book of the Republic of Kazakhstan, printed p. 26; §7 Plants and animals of Kazakhstan's specially protected natural territories, printed p. 27; §8 Animals and plants of the local region entered in the Red Book, printed p. 32; Module 10, printed p. 125; Module 1: Species native to Kazakhstan — photo spread and Table 1.4 matching common names to scientific names (Red-throated loon, Snow leopard, Semirechensk salamander, Shrubby buckwheat…), printed p. 23; built for the Kazakhstan placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kazakhstan-placements.md)
658. **Types of nervous systems — diffuse, nodal and tubular** (`biology/types-of-nervous-systems-diffuse-nodal-and-tubular`) — Animal nervous systems climb an evolutionary ladder: the diffuse nerve net of a hydra, the nodal system of nerve knots and chains in worms and insects, and the tubular system of vertebrates — a cord with a brain at its head end. (Kazakhstan — *BIOLOGY 7 (Sagintayev et al., Astana-kitap), English-language edition (record 1582)*; *БИОЛОГИЯ 7 (Solovyova et al., Atamura, first edition 2017), Kazakh-language edition (record 245)*; *Биология 7 (Ochkur & Kurmanalieva, Mektep, first edition 2017), Kazakh-language edition (record 246)*; *Биология 7 (Ochkur et al., Mektep 2025), Kazakh-language edition (record 1476)*; *Биология 7 (Solovyova, Atamura 2025), Russian-language edition (record 1519)*; *Биология — 7-сынып (NIS Biology, Grade 7), Kazakh-language edition (record 1039)*, student textbook, §7.2 Types of nervous systems, printed p. 78; §35 Types of nervous systems, printed p. 153; §37 Comparison of the types of nervous systems of animals, printed p. 181; 10.1 Types of nervous system, printed p. 148; §35 Types of nervous system, printed p. 128; §39 Types of nervous system of animals, printed p. 160; built for the Kazakhstan placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kazakhstan-placements.md)
659. **Stress, sleep and mental health** (`biology/stress-sleep-and-mental-health`) — Mental health is held by sleep, biological rhythms and a workable day: stress strains the nervous system, sleep restores work capacity, and the hygiene of sleep and work — regular hours, rest and routine — protects both. (Kazakhstan — *BIOLOGY 7 (Sagintayev et al., Astana-kitap), English-language edition (record 1582)*; *БИОЛОГИЯ 7 (Solovyova et al., Atamura, first edition 2017), Kazakh-language edition (record 245)*; *Биология 7 (Ochkur & Kurmanalieva, Mektep, first edition 2017), Kazakh-language edition (record 246)*; *Биология 7 (Ochkur et al., Mektep 2025), Kazakh-language edition (record 1476)*; *Биология 7 (Solovyova, Atamura 2025), Russian-language edition (record 1519)*; *Биология — 7-сынып (NIS Biology, Grade 7), Kazakh-language edition (record 1039)*, student textbook, §7.3 The guarantee of strong (mental) health, printed p. 80; §44 Biological rhythms. Sleep, printed p. 184; §45 Hygiene of sleep and work capacity, printed p. 186; §46 The importance of sleep for the human organism. Biological rhythms, printed p. 209; §47 Work capacity. Daily routine, printed p. 213; 10.8 Sleep and biorhythm, printed p. 162; 10.9 Workability. Daily regimen. Work hygiene, printed p. 164; 10.10 Mental health and stress, printed p. 166; §44 Biological rhythms. Sleep, printed p. 155; §45 Sleep hygiene and work capacity, printed p. 159; §46 Daily regime, hygiene of mental work, printed p. 162; §47 The importance of sleep for restoring the organism's life activity, printed p. 185; §48 Rules for maintaining normal mental health, printed p. 189; built for the Kazakhstan placements, expansion-biology-2026-11-13/kazakhstan-placements.md)
